| # | Test Name | What It Tests | Steps | Summary | Scope | Source Code |
|---|-----------|---------------|-------|---------|-------|-------------|
| 742 | Validate_WithFutureDate_ReturnsValid | Validator accepts a date in the future | Arrange future date -> Act Validate -> Assert result is valid | Verifies a future date passes the past-date check | PastDateValidator.Validate; in-scope: future-date acceptance | [L21](https://github.com/Zocdoc/interop-availability-api/blob/2a20858a2ba1266645f8a3f47c67e4d08a9e673f/tests/UnitTests/Web/Validation/PastDateValidatorTests.cs#L21) |
| 743 | Validate_WithPastDate_ReturnsInvalid | Validator rejects a date in the past | Arrange past date -> Act Validate -> Assert result is invalid | Verifies a past date fails the past-date check | PastDateValidator.Validate; in-scope: past-date rejection | [L35](https://github.com/Zocdoc/interop-availability-api/blob/2a20858a2ba1266645f8a3f47c67e4d08a9e673f/tests/UnitTests/Web/Validation/PastDateValidatorTests.cs#L35) |
